**Rolling window:** 2026-09-29 through 2026-10-05

## Best date plans

### 2026-09-30 — Bhangra in the Plaza + Bar à Part

**Bhangra in the Plaza, then dinner.** French wine bar and cafe at Bar à Part. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Herald+Square+area%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&destination=72+Bedford+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10014&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://34thstreet.org/calendar/event/bhangra-in-the-plaza/2026-09-30) · [Restaurant](https://www.zimmisnyc.com/)

### 2026-10-02 — Greenwich Village Gargoyles, Grotesques and Mascarons + Bar à Part

**Greenwich Village Gargoyles, Grotesques and Mascarons, then dinner.** French wine bar and cafe at Bar à Part. It is a 9-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 3 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=561+LaGuardia+Place%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10012&destination=72+Bedford+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10014&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.thevillagetrip.com/event/greenwich-village-gargoyles-grotesques-and-mascarons/) · [Restaurant](https://www.zimmisnyc.com/)

### 2026-09-30 — iara + A Light That Never Goes Out

**Dinner, then A Light That Never Goes Out.** Brazilian at iara. It is 1.9 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. A Light That Never Goes Out starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=205+Allen+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10002&destination=58+Park+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10016&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.scandinaviahouse.org/event/a-light-that-never-goes-out-new-nordic-cinema/) · [Restaurant](https://iara.nyc/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/iara)

### 2026-09-30 — East Village Underground: Two Centuries of Creativity and Rebellion + iara

**East Village Underground: Two Centuries of Creativity and Rebellion, then dinner.** Brazilian at iara. It is a 9-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 3 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=131+East+10th+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&destination=205+Allen+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10002&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.thevillagetrip.com/event/east-village-underground-two-centuries-of-creativity-and-rebellion-walking-tour-2026/) · [Restaurant](https://iara.nyc/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/iara)

### 2026-10-01 — Beatnik Greenwich Village walking tour + Bar CC

**Beatnik Greenwich Village walking tour, then dinner.** Cocktail bar and American bar food at Bar CC. It is a 16-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 3 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Corner+of+West+8th+and+MacDougal+Streets%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10011&destination=85+Tenth+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10011&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.thevillagetrip.com/event/beatnik-greenwich-village-walking-tour-2026/) · [Restaurant](https://ny.eater.com/news/413619/nyc-restaurant-news-september-2026)

### 2026-10-01 — The History of Concrete with John Wilson Q&A + Sofreh

**The History of Concrete with John Wilson Q&A, then dinner.** Persian at Sofreh. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6:30 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=188+Prospect+Park+West%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=75+St+Marks+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/movies/the-history-of-concrete/) · [Restaurant](https://www.sofrehnyc.com/)

### 2026-09-30 — Gather Round: Let's Dance at Manhattan West + Bar CC

**Gather Round: Let's Dance at Manhattan West, then dinner.** Cocktail bar and American bar food at Bar CC. It is a 17-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 5 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=385+9th+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&destination=85+Tenth+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10011&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://manhattanwestnyc.com/event/gather-round-lets-dance-2026-2/2026-09-30/) · [Restaurant](https://ny.eater.com/news/413619/nyc-restaurant-news-september-2026)

### 2026-10-01 — Cafe Commerce + Ludovico Einaudi: Solo Piano

**Dinner, then Ludovico Einaudi: Solo Piano.** American at Cafe Commerce. It is an 18-minute walk from the venue. Doors 7 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=964+Lexington+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10021&destination=881+Seventh+Avenue%2C+New+York+City%2C+NY+10019&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.axs.com/events/1320931/ludovico-einaudi-solo-piano-tickets) · [Event booking](https://www.carnegiehall.org/calendar/2026/10/01/ludovico-einaudi-0800pm) · [Restaurant](https://www.cafecommercenyc.com/)

## Where to eat

Checked the candidates against captured restaurant/menu evidence and limited public follow-up for the highest-risk claims; keep the source-specific leads, but demote ordinary menus/restaurants and remove unsupported deal/new-opening language.

Dining research: 2026-09-29T10:35:07+00:00

No candidate should claim a Michelin star or Michelin award for a New York restaurant. Eater’s Manhattan heatmap says the Mexico City original El Califa de León was the world’s first Michelin-starred taqueria in 2024, but the NYC outpost is only treated here as an August 2026 opening. Direct Michelin Guide pages returned no usable content in the public web reader, so no new Michelin claims were added.
